- **aid-skills-auto-load-by-context**: Wire in the AIDE orchestrator to auto-load the right skill (SOP) into every model prompt based on detected task type — the "fluid model" pattern from the 2026 research. - **aida-marketing**: AIDA 마케팅 프레임워크. 광고 카피, 랜딩 페이지, 이메일 마케팅, 제품 설명을 작성할 때 사용합니다. '광고', '마케팅', '랜딩페이지', '제품 소개', '판매' 요청 시 적용. ([videa-edtech/careerone-chatbot](https://github.com/videa-edtech/careerone-chatbot/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/aida-marketing) · 2 copies)
- **aida-plan**: Pick the readers and top tasks for a new app, lay out the help site, and produce outline cards for review. Use after aida-discover has mapped the app and aida-understand has written the brief, or when re-planning site structure after a big feature change. High-stakes, low-frequency — rare escalation target for aida-repair too. ([tryaida/aida](https://github.com/tryaida/aida/tree/HEAD/skills/aida-plan) · ★10)
- **aida-repair**: Fix one walkthrough after a failed capture (broken locator, changed label, failed check) and re-run capture for just that walkthrough. Reads only the trimmed failure report, not the full page or logs; stops and reports a suspected app bug or environment problem instead of editing. Escalates to aida-plan's deep tier after two same-step same-cause failures or 5 total attempts. Use when AIDA capture reports a failure, or CI flags a walkthrough as broken. ([tryaida/aida](https://github.com/tryaida/aida/tree/HEAD/skills/aida-repair) · ★10)
- **aida-review**: Review finished AIDA output — page text tone, screenshots, the QA report, and personal-data risk — without seeing the reasoning that produced it. Runs in a fresh-context subagent so it judges the result, not the author's own justification. Use before any publish, and whenever a person asks "does this look right". ([tryaida/aida](https://github.com/tryaida/aida/tree/HEAD/skills/aida-review) · ★10)
- **aida-understand**: Write the app brief (app-brief.md) — what the app is for, who uses it and the job they're doing, the things it keeps track of, its words, rules, rhythm, and tasks that start from more than one screen. Use after aida-discover and before aida-plan on a new app, or when the brief is missing, stale, or fails aida brief check. ([tryaida/aida](https://github.com/tryaida/aida/tree/HEAD/skills/aida-understand) · ★10)
- **aida-write**: Write one walkthrough's YAML from an approved outline card, in plain words using on-screen names, then validate and capture it. Runs as one subagent per walkthrough, in parallel with others. Use after aida-plan's outline is approved, when a person asks to change one specific walkthrough's steps or wording, or when AIDA ci flags a new route no walkthrough covers (tier "new") with its exact draft command. ([tryaida/aida](https://github.com/tryaida/aida/tree/HEAD/skills/aida-write) · ★10)
- **aida:fix**: AIDA Fix - Fix existing project to meet all quality gates. Takes a partially-built project and iterates until all 19 gates pass. Useful for completing interrupted implementations or improving test coverage. ([clearclown/claude-code-aida](https://github.com/clearclown/claude-code-aida/tree/HEAD/skills/fix) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **aida:resume**: AIDA Resume - Continue from last session state. Reads session.json and resumes from where AIDA left off. Useful for continuing interrupted implementations. ([clearclown/claude-code-aida](https://github.com/clearclown/claude-code-aida/tree/HEAD/skills/resume) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **aidabench-eval**: Evaluates LLM-driven document analysis agents on end-to-end data analytics workflows, including question answering, data visualization, and file generation. Use when the user wants to benchmark on AIDABench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Pass@3. - **aidaily**: 整理和汇总 AI 领域最新资讯的 skill。当用户说出"帮我整理今日的ai资讯"、"整理每日ai资讯"、 "今天有什么ai新闻"、"获取ai日报"等类似话语时触发。自动从配置的 AI 博客源获取最新文章， 生成卡片式 HTML 日报。支持用户指定其他资讯源 URL，支持将结果发送到 webhook 或邮箱， 支持语言切换（默认中文，可指定英文等）。 必须使用此 skill 处理任何与 AI 资讯整理、AI 日报生成、AI 新闻汇总相关的请求。 ([Matys1009/aidaily-skill](https://github.com/Matys1009/aidaily-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
